Accepted unproven observations remain listed; an automatic stress trigger is not restored as part of success.

#### Deliberate RALPLAN-DR summary

**Principles (four).** (1) Repair the observed boundary, not the whole protocol design. (2) Bound resource retention while preserving authentication, last-sender routing and fail-closed input handling. (3) Evidence labels distinguish defect reproduction, new policy rejection, fixture sensitivity and existing-behavior certification. (4) Fail-closed CI exceptions are exact and visible; manual scheduling must not erase required evidence.

**Decision drivers (top three).** (1) Smallest defensible changes to the p47 security/evidence follow-ups. (2) Fast serial landing throughput while retaining all 17 baseline checks and adding the required netem context (18 thereafter). (3) Honest portability/load evidence across local macOS, remote Linux and hosted Windows/macOS jobs.

| Viable option | Bounded pros | Bounded cons |
|---|---|---|
| **Focused four-lane closeout, chosen**: bounded WG, DNS wording, test stabilisation/coverage, exact skip enforcement, isolated automatic netem | Fits disjoint package ownership; fixes concrete retention/lock risks; keeps performance floor and current architecture; CI pressure limited | LRU is an intentional finite-retention deviation; synchronous log caller still can stall; native user evidence remains absent; one hosted stress receipt costs runner time |
| Two serial implementation lanes for all units, same fixes and gates | Less coordination/ownership overhead; fewer simultaneous load sources; same acceptance possible | WG, H3 and CI authors block each other; cannot prepare disjoint candidates concurrently; lead queue remains the same bottleneck |
| Count unanswered DNS only and retain netem in required CI with a relaxed hosted floor | Closer literal “unanswered” wording; performance visible on every PR | Need a separately bounded requestless-flow store; extra state complexity; lowering 80% does not fix the direct-await timeout and weakens the chosen requirement |

The third option is viable as a future redesign but not selected for this short round; it needs a new retained-state contract and does not address the measured netem failure mechanism. A new self-hosted netem fleet is deferred: operational setup is outside this unit and no confirmed dedicated runner is part of the approved premises.

#### Three-scenario pre-mortem

1. **Unlocking logs changes counters but still blocks all socket reads.** Mitigation attached to unit 1: preserve exact counters/log schedule; gated-handler parent/tip test asserts registration progress, not packet-reader throughput. Explicitly report residual synchronous logging; no false availability claim or new logger subsystem.
2. **LRU or FIN coverage masks a product regression.** Mitigation attached to units 3/4: eviction at 4097 and last-sender/rejected-input rows, encrypted two-peer e2e, incomplete-request reset plus server/client FIN/sibling/receipt rows. Historical parent overlays touch tests only; no production mutation is passed off as red-on-parent.
3. **CI becomes green by broad skip exceptions or hidden measurement retries.** Mitigation attached to units 2/5/7 and lead N14: exact identity+reason acceptance with negative fixtures and real CI planted-unknown rejection; unchanged heap and netem bounds; measurement-sensitivity rows; first-attempt/retry artifacts; complete final manual workflow receipt. Setup/restore failure never gets a retry; cancelled rows never count.

#### Expanded test plan

| Level | Scope | Mechanically required evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Unit | Demux locked-drop sites, LRU victim/refresh/IPv4+IPv6, peer-count 255/256, prefix helper, DNS retained-entry definition | Exact state/count/error assertions; parent-red for lock/LRU, policy-red for count, fixture/certification labels for helper/DNS |
| Integration | Authenticated WG two-peer routing; real H3 client/server FIN; DNS TCP/UDP; HTTP/1 100 MiB upload/response | Exact-head baseline plus Linux/macOS proxy integration, WireGuard native CI rows; real peers and cleanup joins |
| Count-based stress | WG package count50; gated/route race rows count200; H3 package count200 and late-DATA count600; httplayer upstream count50; dnslayer count200; streaming non-race count200 | **Use the exact long-count command matrix and its explicit test/containing budgets**, including H3 90m/105m, dnslayer60m/75m and GC streaming90m/105m with calibration margin. `-cpu=1,2,8` =>150/600/1800 repetitions as applicable; remote Debian streaming count20/umask022. Exit codes, complete tallies, SHA/host/toolchain and digests required. Timeout/partial logs never count. Expensive final verification may say audit-from-receipts, not repeated |
| Harness / CI | Exact allowlists, interleaved JSON, malformed input, pipeline exit; netem-only/default partition and bounded retry restoration | BSD awk+gawk, bash syntax, shellcheck/actionlint, planted unknown CI FAIL then clean candidate CI PASS, every required context preserved |
| Hosted end-to-end evidence | Entire manual Stress workflow at final head, netem-only Linux measurement, all existing race manifest rows on supported platforms | One uncancelled run id/headSha; each row/job result; exact-head artifacts; attempts visible; N14 rows1/3/4 complete |
| Fuzz | Existing DNS real-socket fuzz targets and unchanged protocol targets | Local DNS bounded200x after measured drain; fresh exact-head 30s CI fuzz-smoke; no new broad campaign |
| Observability | Drop counters/power-of-two logs, skip class/accepted/rejected summaries, heap baseline/live peak, netem durations/ratio/qdisc/restore, late-frame Reset cause | Logs prove the asserted boundary and expose first failures; no keys, env dumps or raw credentials; original unproven observations retained |

#### ADR — focused closeout follow-ups

- **Decision:** run the four-lane closeout above, with M1 first; use 4096-source LRU and <=255 keys; document DNS retained-entry semantics; force GC before live-heap samples without loosening 50 MiB; certify both H3 endpoint roles and isolate the text prefix; enforce exact skips; move unchanged netem comparison to a separate automatic required Linux Network gates job with one visible measurement retry; dispatch final hosted stress evidence.
- **Drivers:** bounded risk repair; fast gated landings; honest cross-platform/load evidence.
- **Alternatives considered:** two serial lanes; unanswered-only DNS plus relaxed required hosted netem; a new dedicated self-hosted netem runner.
- **Why chosen:** preserves the current protocol contracts and user decisions with narrow grants. It avoids making a missing-answer counter into a loophole for retained requestless flows and avoids lowering a proxy ratio to address a direct-reference timeout. Four lanes prepare independent work without competing on files.
- **Consequences:** **Routing changes are limited to the approved finite aggregate LRU retention and existing miss fallback.** Eviction can move a previously learned destination back to peer 0 until successful authenticated admission relearns it, and this must be documented. Finite source/key bounds retain the approved printed `10.0.0.n/32` single-/24 address plan. GC-based tests measure live retention, require an acknowledged held-tail sample and perturb timings. A blocking logger still blocks its caller after unlock. Race stress remains manual under the ratified historical “until more of the port is implemented / when the implementation settles” condition; netem remains automatic and required. User native acceptance and filing remain outside completion.
- **Follow-ups:** the stress triggers return when the user directs it (the ratified LD-17 condition, kept by the user at the approval of revision 12 on 2026-10-11; the planner's next-round/no-new-product-finding criterion was not adopted). Revisit NB-15 on a new Go release and NB-23 only on attributable recurrence; retain A2/A3 as user actions. No dependency, transport-close API, source authorization or native process-attribution redesign is implied.

### The hosted-runner hardening round (revision 13)

**Outcome (revision 14): CLOSED — PASS with findings under user waiver A=A1.** All units 1–4 and landings #1–#9 are complete at `bb314668f190d73fb1f4d692dbdfbce4cdfb62f8`. The independent verifier's Addendum 1 FINAL FAIL is retained, not converted to PASS: user decision A=A1 waives the selected native Windows production-Start failure for p49 closeout only. Native run `38145814507` remains failed (3/480 descendant executions); NB-32 carries the production repair to revision 14. The twenty final-head Linux runs completed with 40 HTTP3 terminal receipts; NB-38 carries the two direct-netem first-attempt timeouts to diagnosis. §3.4 records the amendments, closures and evidence. The remaining revision 13 text is execution history, including its original frozen scope and subsequently amended selector wording; it is not unfinished work or p50's grant.

**Historical context.** p49 started from immutable main `2de7987`, after revision 12 completed. It hardened test acquisition and hosted evidence, not P5 or a protocol redesign. Windows failures are retained at `.omc/artifacts/p48-session/ci-logs/main-2de7987-test-windows-114388712409.log:1964-1980` (NB-29) and `rulings-p48.md:80-82` (NB-27). The raw logs prove access-denied/exhaustion; the UDP-excluded-block mechanism was the lead's recorded attribution, not independently established from those earlier port numbers (`reviews/verify-p48.md:225-228`). The final native p49 receipt captured actual per-protocol excluded ranges and established the production-path attribution; its failure is retained.

**Objectives / must have.** Shared bounded Windows-aware acquisition; same-number TCP/UDP pairing and family preservation; deterministic forbidden-range/error sensitivity; real sockets retained through ownership transfer; exact-head native Windows fixture runs; unchanged performance thresholds and failed-attempt retention; precise completion/rerun ledger wording. **Must not have.** Port-number-only release/rebind as the sole safety mechanism; larger retry budget as the only fix; new skips/allowlist exemptions; fixed-port-only fixtures; production DNS/mode/QUIC changes; dependency/toolchain bumps; numeric throughput relaxation or HTTP/3 retry policy; restoration/upload bypass; restored Stress triggers; workers handling A2/A3/N13. Plan identifiers, unit numbers, NB/LD/R identifiers and round/lane names never appear in source, tests, comments, workflows, scripts or commit messages.

#### Source truth and choice

The resolver fixture chooses TCP `:0`, tries UDP on its port, retains rejected TCP listeners and exhausts 64 candidates; WSAEACCES 10013 is retryable only on Windows (`go:addons/dnsresolver/authoritative_pair_test.go:16-51`), and `authoritativeServer` uses that helper without exclusion knowledge (`network_test.go:25-35`). Existing `freeport` queries cached IPv4 TCP/UDP exclusions (`go:internal/netutil/freeport/excluded_windows.go:21-47`), but paired selection still allocates TCP `:0` on each of 64 iterations and closes both sockets before returning a number (`freeport.go:84-119`). Code does not promise sequential numbers: that is the observed Windows allocation pattern. Merely routing fixtures through the integer API still permits exhaustion and a release/rebind race. The appended p48 correction (`.omc/artifacts/p48-session/rulings-p48.md:188-190`) confirms the actual NB-29 fixture did not call freeport; UDP exclusion discovery already existed and is not a new missing capability. Native range diagnostic exists (`excluded_windows_test.go:8-16`); subprocess fixtures currently cover all-ports exclusions and unreadable output, not a wide blocked interval with an available complement (`windows_excluded_test.go:17-89`).

Remaining DNS fixtures: isolation mode suffix `@127.0.0.1:0` (`go:internal/proxy/modeserver/dns_isolation_test.go:54-56`); standalone IPv4/IPv6 mode port zero (`dns_standalone_test.go:30-50`); reverse UDP origin and mode listener (`dns_test.go:44-48,72-75`); authoritative miekg origin and mode listener (`dns_authoritative_test.go:36-62`); dnslayer UDP session (`go:internal/proxy/layers/dnslayer/dns_test.go:114-140`). Modeserver already has private injectable TCP/UDP bind functions (`modeserver.go:82-83,152-155`) and starts paired transports through `listenBothSockets` (`:207-226`): use those test seams, not a new production listener contract.

**Fixed acquisition decision (R49-1).** The round is fixture-only: add a paired owned-socket API to `freeport`, preserving existing integer APIs and their release-before-return semantics. Freeze a caller-context contract taking explicit network/family, host and requested port and returning a live TCP listener plus live UDP socket on the same address/number, or no sockets and an error. The small unit 1 candidate lands before consumers. For IPv4 pairs, reuse existing cached TCP+UDP discovery and avoid their union; bypass known blocked intervals without charging the 64 candidate-acquisition retry budget. On stale/unreadable discovery, real bind errors remain authoritative; bounded distinct randomized candidates are an allowed fallback. Only requested port zero permits whole-pair reselection; fixed requested ports never change. Every partial pair closes before reselection, and non-collision errors fail fast (Windows 10013 may be treated as the platform's bind-exclusion error, never arbitrary permission errors on other OSes). Discovery remains IPv4-only; IPv6 keeps existing probe/skip behavior, actual IPv6 sockets and identity, with no IPv4-table substitution or new IPv6 discovery scope. UDP-first merely reverses protocol asymmetry; a larger budget alone does not escape known excluded blocks. The live API may release an unneeded paired socket immediately for a single-protocol fixture, retaining ownership of the used socket; no independent TCP/UDP selection may violate shared-port consumers. Production paths in `go:internal/proxy/modeserver/modeserver.go:213-225`, `both.go:14-43` and `packet.go:17-47` share the primitive but stay frozen: this repairs test setup, not product port-zero reliability. Authority: `.omc/artifacts/p49-session/rulings-p49.md:5-12`.

**Selected throughput approach:** carry NB-16/NB-26 as observations and improve diagnostics at existing thresholds. HTTP/2 already has live-peer timeout/protocol separation and locked timeout publication (`go:internal/proxy/layers/httplayer/http2_load_netem_test.go:61-133`); its completed comparison is 256 MiB, 16 MiB windows, ratio >=0.8 (`:233-267`). HTTP/3 uses a 30-second read deadline for 64 MiB and a separate 2-second sibling row (`http3_load_test.go:38-43,249-264,269-314`), in a one-minute session context (`:57-58`). The recorded stall's root cause remains unproven (R48-6, `rulings-p48.md:60-78`). Environment-scaled deadline changes weaken an absolute gate; the h2 classifier hard-codes HTTP/2 phases/bytes/windows (`go:hack/netem-gate.sh:15-75`), so routing HTTP/3 through it is not a mechanical reuse. No new policy is inferred from a later green run.

#### Task flow and exclusive ownership

**Maximize parallel preparation:** start freeport (unit 1), throughput-evidence (unit 3) and docs (unit 4) together on day one. The three disjoint unit 2 consumers—dnsresolver-fixtures, dns-mode-fixtures and dns-layer-fixtures—may inventory/prepare tests read-only immediately and begin writes in parallel the moment unit 1's small API+contract candidate lands. Their exclusive package/test-file sets prevent shared fixture-helper writes. Thus three lanes author immediately and up to six author/documentation lanes operate after API landing; no lane waits for an unrelated unit. Each lane commits and pushes per small coherent task, not one accumulated round/unit blob; keep at most one pushed unlanded candidate per lane and do not rewrite an approved head. Docs starts p48-history/current-policy reconciliation independently and finishes source-dependent wording after accepted unit 2/3 handoffs. A seventh, independent windows-evidence author may prepare the granted manual producer from day one; it lands before final freeze and supplies only the round-end count20 gate. API freeze/serial gate and hosted queue, not authoring, are bottlenecks; no concurrent heavy campaigns.

| Order | Unit / owning lane | Exclusive files and scope | Dependency / candidate shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Additive paired owned-socket API / freeport | `internal/netutil/freeport/` new paired helper/tests plus existing exclusion primitives only as required; integer APIs/observable contracts preserved | Starts day one; small API+focused contract candidate first, then optional independent coverage task; sole package writer |

#### Deliverables, acceptance and evidence

**1. Additive paired acquisition — frozen API.** Add `freeport.ListenPair(ctx context.Context, network, address string) (net.Listener, *net.UDPConn, error)`, with `network` exactly `tcp4` or `tcp6` and the matching UDP family derived internally; `address` is an explicit family-compatible host plus requested numeric port. The concrete UDP result supports the resolver's `ReadFromUDP`/`WriteToUDP` (`go:addons/dnsresolver/network_test.go:63,71`) and is assignable to modeserver's `net.PacketConn` seam. Return live same-host/port TCP+UDP objects or nil/nil plus an error, never a closed prior candidate. Caller owns both until each actual handoff; a single-protocol fixture closes its unused mate. Test seam adapters validate network/family/host/requested-port arguments, consume each handle once and never silently ignore a mismatch. Fixed requested ports get **one candidate acquisition** and no reselection; bind refusal returns a typed `*net.OpError`, retaining wrapped cause with `errors.Is`/`errors.As`. Port-zero selection has **64 candidate acquisition attempts**, not 64 combined syscalls: record TCP and UDP bind tallies independently (a 64-pair campaign may perform 64 TCP and 64 UDP binds). Known-excluded candidates are bypassed before acquisition and consume none of these 64 attempts; the complement search itself is finite. On every failed attempt/error/cancellation release all partial handles before whole-pair reselection; successful handles remain live and usable until caller/handoff closes them. Existing integer API code/results, released-port semantics, fixed-address/non-Windows native bind behavior and production listener paths stay unchanged.

**Candidate domain / discovery.** Windows IPv4 explicit complement/randomized fallback uses only the OS-reported TCP/UDP dynamic-port range intersection minus both excluded sets, never arbitrary valid or privileged ports. Native range bounds are cached once, with finite context-aware acquisition; if bounds are unavailable, do not guess an explicit port—use bounded native `:0` candidates/bind checks and record domain-unavailable fallback. Non-Windows and IPv6 continue native ephemeral/fixed-address binding semantics; no Windows IPv4 table/domain is applied to IPv6. Exclusion discovery remains the existing process-wide cache's **two sequential background-context 30-second netsh queries** (`go:internal/netutil/freeport/excluded_windows.go:21-39`), at most 60 seconds of command budgets, not per fixture. The live API's caller waits cancellation-aware on one shared bounded discovery completion, never an unbounded per-caller goroutine; cancellation returns promptly with no sockets even if first discovery continues to its bound and caches its terminal result. Any added native dynamic-range bounds query has a separate explicit finite caller-context budget recorded in the API handoff (maximum 5 seconds per protocol, cached once); it adds no IPv6 excluded-range discovery. Certify a stalled first-discovery primitive: cancel caller, assert prompt context error/no binds/no sockets, release/join the bounded cache worker and verify subsequent callers consume its completed cache, not a leaked duplicate process.

Acceptance: deterministic paired rows exercise TCP-only, UDP-only and overlapping IPv4 exclusions, an excluded interval wider than 64 with usable complement, empty/all-excluded tables, command/parse failure, stale knowledge followed by wrapped WSAEACCES 10013, real occupied-port collisions, unrelated fatal errors, fixed-port refusal without reselection, caller cancellation and actual IPv6 identity. Assert shared address/number, outside both readable IPv4 tables, no retry budget consumed on known ranges, <=64 candidate acquisitions with separate <=64 TCP/<=64 UDP bind tallies (not <=64 combined syscalls), whole-pair rollback before retry, fixed-address one-attempt typed cause, stalled-discovery caller cancellation without orphan workers, finite domain/candidate/reservation work, exactly-once partial close and live caller-owned usable sockets at success. Existing integer APIs stay preservation: `go:internal/netutil/freeport/reuse_test.go:49-98` and `bind_error_test.go:62-80` are already-green release/cause contracts, not new parent-red claims. Use map test cases, `t.Context`, go-cmp and injected bind/range primitives for selector logic; actual protocol peers remain real.

Evidence: same deterministic forbidden-interval/error inputs and expectations through test-only adapters: parent adapter drives existing `listenAuthoritativePair` callbacks on unchanged `2de7987`; candidate adapter drives the additive owned-pair API. Inject wrapped excluded-range access errors for allocator-chosen blocked candidates and allow explicit usable complement; parent exhausts, candidate succeeds. Retain both adapters/commands and explain the adapter difference; never mutate parent production, cite a missing API/compile failure as red, or claim unchanged legacy FreePort now repairs the interval. Original Windows logs stand in for native parent-red. Local freeport focused regression `-race -count=200 -cpu=1,2,8`, integer-API preservation suite and exact-head native Windows subprocess contracts; targeted gopls/default+Windows vet/build and all fresh required checks. IPv4 range diagnostics capture actual tables. New API green certification covers fatal/error/cancel/ownership paths; no parent-red claim for already-passing old release/cause rows. Keep unit 1's first commit to API+focused contracts so unit 2 starts immediately after its exact-head green landing; optional additional coverage is a separate small candidate.

**2. DNS fixture adoption.** Remove ad hoc repeated same-port allocation from authoritativePair in favor of unit 1 live acquisition, preserving its typed error sensitivity and TCP-fallback behavior. Acquire real origin sockets through the shared helper; configure authoritative servers using owned listeners/packet sockets after verifying the pinned miekg API. For modeserver fixtures, inject already-acquired sockets through existing private listen seams before `Start`, preserving same-port pairing and existing binding assertions; do not pick/release a numeric port and rely on luck at `Start`. Before `Start`, the fixture owns both live objects. Each validated seam invocation transfers exactly the matching object; transferred objects are cleaned by the instance's existing failure/Stop mechanics, and the fixture closes any not-yet-transferred mate on **every failed Start**, then clears adapter references so no retry returns a closed handle. Certify failure before any transfer, after TCP transfer/before UDP transfer, after both transfers/failed factory activation and after successful Start/Stop; cancellation/fatal setup releases all remaining handles and joins workers. Use concrete *net.UDPConn directly for resolver datagrams or an explicit checked adapter; no unchecked consumer assertion. IPv6 availability retains its existing probe/skip and actual ::1/tcp6/udp6 identity; no IPv4 exclusion tables/domain or new skip acceptance applies.

Acceptance: CNAME/validation/TCP-fallback rows pass; standalone IPv4/IPv6 TCP/UDP have matching request/response identity and content; reverse authoritative and malformed-tuple isolation still prove origin delivery, unrelated tuple survival and lifecycle cleanup. UDP layer admission/framing/retention rows stay unchanged. Force a denied first acquisition then usable complement, verify all denied/acquired sockets close on failed Start, Stop, cancellation and fatal setup. Inspect every bind in the granted DNS files and document intentional fixed-port/negative-test exclusions; no ordinary ephemeral DNS origin/listener bypass remains. No new skip, t.Parallel-based contention workaround, larger deadline or weakened byte/hook assertion.

Evidence: original NB-27/NB-29 hosted failure logs stand in for native parent-red; deterministic injected-error paired selection provides red/green without changing parent production. Local **socket-heavy DNS fixture** rows use `-race -count=20 -cpu=1,2,8` (60 per identity), preserving the current dnslayer socket-stress guidance (`go:internal/proxy/layers/dnslayer/dns_test.go:36-38`), with 60m Go /75m post-lock shell budgets; pure selector sensitivities keep unit 1's count200. Run two fresh whole-tree race/proxy-integration gates serially under the lock. A named `windows-evidence` author owns an additive manual-only `.github/workflows/windows-fixture-evidence.yaml` producer, granted now as part of unit 4 evidence preparation and landed with its own fresh required runs **before final SHA freeze**. It adds no PR-required context or automatic trigger and does not edit Stress. Once unit 1, all unit 2 consumers, diagnostics, producer and final docs land, lead freezes the immutable round head; credential-safe lead routing dispatches the producer on that resolved head. The job uses `windows-2025`, module-selected toolchain, verified current action pins, `go test -race -count=20 -cpu=1,2,8 -timeout=60m` focused freeport plus every migrated DNS fixture identity (60 executions per selected ordinary identity), with 75m containing job margin including setup/upload. Enforce a 60-minute aggregate test-step cutoff across all serial package invocations, including process-tree termination/cleanup within that cutoff; each binary retains its existing `-timeout=60m`, counts and deadlines unchanged. Inside the 75-minute job, bound aggregate setup to 8 minutes and evidence finalization plus `always()` upload to 5 minutes, leaving 2 minutes of job-overhead reserve so the job timeout does not consume the upload window. Interrupted package/identity counts are INCOMPLETE: retain partial logs and the interruption status, never relabel them as completed counts or a lower-count pass. Producer refuses an empty/missing intended test set, records actual head/run/job checkout, expanded package/pattern/tag/count/cpu sets, per-identity passes/fails/skips and real IPv4 exclusion/domain tables, and archives logs plus SHA256SUMS even on failure. Only fresh native execution of migrated fixtures plus deterministic selection/ownership proof closes NB-27/NB-29, never an unrelated green rerun.

**3. Throughput evidence gathering only.** Binding R49-1 item 4, verbatim:

> 4. Unit 3 (HTTP/3 and h2 throughput rows) is evidence-gathering only: thresholds, windows, deadlines and the netem classifier unchanged; every HTTP/3 terminal path emits one receipt before cleanup (phase, expected and actual bytes, elapsed, last-progress offset, typed terminal cause); job names and the 18 required contexts unchanged; NB-16/NB-26 stay open until a classified recurrence; the disposition trigger for the next round is written in the plan.

Shared fatal helper paths (`go:internal/proxy/layers/httplayer/http3_test.go:168-202`, `quicgo_routing_test.go:45-95`) and setup/worker failures must record original error/phase before `t.Fatal`/`t.Errorf` or closure. No production grant.

**Frozen terminal schema/order:** exactly one receipt for success and each setup/handshake/warmup/request/frame/body/read/write/context/worker terminal failure, before cleanup. Fields: phase/stream (explicit unavailable before creation), expected bytes, **actual read bytes**, separately **validated payload bytes**, elapsed, last-progress offset, original typed terminal cause, available client/origin QUIC state (unavailable explicit). First causal typed error has precedence over cleanup's later origin 0x100 close (`:124-126,173`); cleanup evidence cannot replace it. Retain bounded framing buffer/no-body-retention (`:210-246`). **No per-chunk logging, progress polling goroutine or added measured-interval blocking/clock campaign**. No duplicate/deferred cleanup receipt replaces the causal receipt. All prior byte/header/sibling/intercept/lifecycle assertions stay; no error tolerance, environment budget, classifier edit or automatic HTTP/3 retry. Certification runs controlled subprocesses for complete fields, exactly-once emission on success/fatal setup/helper/worker paths, partial `n>0` plus error, stalled live peer and early typed close; assert publication precedes cleanup 0x100 and that actual/validated bytes differ correctly. Preserve non-load shared-helper callers with focused existing rows and confirm no polling/logging was inserted into measured intervals. Diagnostic sensitivity is passing certification, not candidate deliberate red. Existing h2 protocol-veto/publication sensitivity and shell contracts remain preservation. HTTP/2 history and HTTP/3 observations have separate denominators: the older h2 failures predate its isolated job, and one recorded h3 failure plus green rerun establishes no failure rate (p48 `rulings-p48.md:60-78`). Fresh exact-head automatic Network receipt includes HTTP/3 64 MiB/30s and sibling 4 MiB/2s, plus independent netem first/retry receipts with sizes/window/ratio/producer/tee/restoration/upload disposition; HTTP/3 receipts stay outside NETEM transcript parsing. Do not combine them into one one-minute session. Netem uses existing automatic exact-head job evidence; do not demand a failure solely to obtain retry artifacts. **Next-round disposition trigger:** the first new exact-head recurrence of either throughput failure with its complete original terminal/measurement receipt becomes a numbered owned finding. Distinguish no-start, progressing/stalled live transfer and protocol/connection termination, preserve partial bytes/elapsed/original cause plus available runner/QUIC state, and write a scoped next-round diagnosis/remediation unit before proposing any deadline, threshold or retry change. A missing/ambiguous receipt is an evidence-completeness defect, not a throughput classification. #### Deliberate RALPLAN-DR summary

**Principles (four):** (1) Repair the observed fixture boundary with reusable acquisition, not product protocols. (2) Preserve socket ownership, finite work and fail-fast unknown errors. (3) Keep numeric gates and evidence labels honest; rerun is not repair. (4) Fresh exact-head hosted checks and independent approval establish completion.

**Drivers (top three):** (1) Prevent repeat Windows TCP/UDP exclusion failures without skips. (2) Protect throughput/skip/restoration contracts while diagnosing hosted variance. (3) Minimize required-check/gate queue cost with disjoint preparation and targeted native evidence.

| Viable option | Bounded pros | Bounded cons |
|---|---|---|
| **Three day-one source/docs lanes, three DNS consumers after API, separate evidence producer (chosen)** | Implements fixed additive API/fixture-only and diagnostics-only premises; units 1/3/docs start together and all DNS packages migrate in parallel after API landing | Up to seven source/docs/evidence contexts/candidates; shared gate/Windows queue remains serial; package evidence must be joined at final head |
| Five source/docs lanes plus evidence producer: same three day-one lanes, then resolver consumer plus one combined mode/layer consumer | Same fixed API, policy and acceptance; fewer handoffs, concurrent resolver versus mode preparation | Mode/layer authoring serialized despite disjoint packages; slower preparation, less parallelism than user requested |
| Four source/docs lanes plus evidence producer: same day-one lanes, one DNS consumer after API | Least coordination; same verifiable fixture and terminal-receipt contracts | All three DNS packages authored serially; underuses disjoint ownership and provides no gate-queue advantage |

R49-1 fixes the technical boundary, so these are viable orchestration options **within** the ratified shape, not an invitation to reverse it. Duplicated per-package acquisition is invalidated by the fixed shared paired-owned API; a new HTTP/3 retry/deadline policy is invalidated for this round by its evidence-only premise.

Runner-aware deadline scaling is not selected: it changes absolute acceptance based on ambient env and does not identify stalled/protocol-failed peers. UDP-first and budget-only changes do not eliminate asymmetric excluded blocks. No new self-hosted runner fleet is assumed.

#### Three-scenario pre-mortem and expanded tests

1. **Selection passes but release/rebind loses the port or another protocol still refuses it.** Live ownership/same-port assertions, family/protocol tables, wrapper seams and native failure logs/range receipts; no number-only success claim.
2. **Fallback loops or closes caller-owned sockets.** Distinct finite candidates/attempt and reservation bounds; all-excluded/unreadable/cancel/fatal injected paths, close tallies and real socket use after return; freeze helper contract before fixture migration.
3. **Diagnostics or reruns silently weaken the performance/CI gate.** Numeric comparison/required-context diff, negative reset/body/cancellation tests, existing netem protocol veto/upload/restoration contracts, retained failures and independent verifier; no env deadline change/new HTTP3 retry/general cancellation waiver.

| Level | Required evidence |
|---|---|
| Unit | Exclusion union/family/bounds/error/cancel/ownership selection, wrapped 10013 parent regression, HTTP3 terminal diagnostic sensitivity; all new tests passing on candidate |
| Integration | Actual authoritative TCP/UDP fallback and DNS hooks/tuple isolation on all supported transports; socket owners close/join; full-tree race and proxy integration; unchanged seven Network core scenarios/Autobahn |
| Hosted / E2E | Round-end frozen-head native Windows producer count20/cpu1,2,8 (60 per identity) with tables/tallies/digests; required count1 CI is baseline only; local counts separately labelled; exact-head automatic HTTP3/netem measurements; immutable head/checkout ids, required 18 check identities, complete counts/digests |
| Fuzz / preservation | Existing automatic fuzz-smoke remains; NB-28 is infrastructure-only policy, never seeded-completion substitution for fuzz duration; no new broad campaign/skip class |
| Observability | Candidate/range/fallback counts without secrets, received bytes/last phase/elapsed/original typed cause, unavailable fields explicit, original failed and retry receipts retained, namespace restoration and upload exits |

#### ADR — hosted-runner hardening

- **Decision:** implement R49-1's additive paired-owned API, fixture-only DNS consumers, evidence-only throughput receipts and docs/ledger reconciliation. Units 1/3/docs start together; three DNS consumer lanes start writes immediately after the small API landing; each commits/pushes per small task.
- **Drivers:** portable paired fixture ownership; unchanged numerical/enforcement/evidence policy; maximum disjoint authoring concurrency with a serial resource gate.
- **Alternatives considered:** six-, five- and four-source/docs-lane shapes plus their separately owned evidence producer, implementing the same fixed technical contract. Per-package duplicate acquisition, new HTTP3 retry and env-scaled deadline are invalidated for this round by R49-1.
- **Why chosen:** resolver, modeserver and dnslayer fixture files are disjoint, so parallel consumers can use one frozen API without shared-file contention. Existing integer selection is intentionally unchanged; live pairs avoid its released-port race for test setup. Evidence gathering can proceed independently without relaxing an unexplained deadline.
- **Consequences:** up to seven source/docs/evidence context/candidate streams, but heavy tests/landings serialize. Native Windows validates fixture reliability only; production port-zero paths and IPv6 exclusion discovery are residuals. Existing integer API contracts preserved. NB-16/NB-26 remain open observations until classified recurrence is explicitly dispositioned; no automatic retry, ruleset migration or full unrelated Stress campaign.
- **Follow-ups:** first complete same-head classified throughput recurrence creates a numbered owned next-round diagnosis/remediation unit; ambiguous/missing receipts trigger evidence repair instead. NB-15 requires attributable/new-toolchain evidence, NB-23 attributable recurrence. A2/A3/N13 and LD-17 return remain the user's separate actions. No implied product socket, source-authentication, QUIC lifecycle or native-attribution redesign.

#### 1. Production listener candidate parity (NB-32)

**Source truth and chosen design.** `go:internal/proxy/modeserver/both.go:14-43` binds TCP first using `i.listen`, returns any TCP error immediately, then binds UDP on the selected shared port and retries at most 64 for port-zero retryable UDP failure (or differing TCP port selections). Standalone DNS, reverse DNS and reverse HTTPS use Both; reverse DNS is not UDP-only. `packet.go:17-47` supplies Both's UDP half and the UDP-only binder, but is unchanged this round. `TestWindowsSharedTCPAccessDenied` in `both_udp_retry_windows_test.go:38-60` pins one TCP WSAEACCES attempt and no UDP acquisition. `ListenPair`'s retry/wrapping/host-family behavior is different and is not substituted. Freeport's production imports are standard-library only, so modeserver→freeport is acyclic, but this is its first product consumer (all existing imports are tests). Keep only candidate policy in freeport and expose the smallest typed, cancellation-aware cursor/domain operation before Both integration. Modeserver retains bind order, complete socket-set ownership, private TCP/UDP seams, error wrappers and host/family resolution. Existing ListenPair/legacy callers keep their contracts and discovery caches; no mutable cache exposed, duplicated worker or general listener framework. A lower shared policy package is viable but not required by dependency direction; any extraction moves only candidate policy, never live socket or integer ownership.

**Frozen candidate/ownership contract (R50-1/R50-2).** Only Windows Both requests with requested port zero and a knowable IPv4 family consume explicit candidates. Use readable TCP/UDP dynamic intersection minus both exclusion sets; unavailable bounds use up to 64 distinct randomized ports in 49152–65535 with exclusions only when the existing combined exclusion discovery succeeded. Discovery is all-or-fallback: `excluded_windows.go:23-40` discards BOTH tables when either protocol discovery/parsing fails; successful TCP plus failed UDP does not retain a partial TCP table. Successfully discovered exclusions remain usable when dynamic bounds are unavailable; these are different failure boundaries. No cache refresh/new netsh command; existing exclusion budgets (30s each) and dynamic bounds budgets (5s each), cached process-wide, remain. Known-excluded candidates never consume the 64 socket-set acquisition budget. Cancellation stops waiting for shared discovery and leaves no acquired socket or duplicate worker. TCP failure, including stale wrapped WSAEACCES/10048, still returns immediately with its original cause and no UDP bind; candidate sampling is not permission to retry TCP. Windows UDP uses only the existing native `addrinuse_windows.go` predicate: WSAEADDRINUSE/10048 or WSAEACCES/10013 through errors.Is. Wrapped Windows syscall.EADDRINUSE and ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED are NOT retryable. Non-Windows UDP retains its existing isAddrInUse predicate. Freeport's acquisition retry classifier is NOT exported or consumed by modeserver; the seam exports candidate selection only. Fixed requested ports never select/retry a different number; non-Windows keeps native `:0` and original behavior. Keep hostname/zone identity, generic network strings and existing explicit-host resolution; no pre-bind DNS lookup, pinned address or substitution of ListenPair's explicit-family resolver. The following matrix is frozen by R50-3, not an executor scope choice.

| Windows Both requested-port-zero host | Candidate selection | Preserved binding/residual contract |
|---|---|---|
| Explicit IPv4 literal, net.ParseIP(host).To4() non-nil | Shared exclusion-aware IPv4 candidate | Original literal and generic tcp/udp networks; the demonstrated `dns@127.0.0.1:0` production failure is inside this case |
| Empty host wildcard | Existing tcp4-first path consumes the IPv4 candidate; tcp6 follows its number | Existing unavailable-family and originally-requested-zero fallback decisions remain; different TCP numbers still trigger full rollback/reselection before UDP; no IPv6 exclusion-table claim |
| Explicit IPv6 literal, including zone-bearing address | Native `:0`, no IPv4 candidate lookup | Literal/zone identity and IPv6 product strategy remain unchanged; residual observation |
| Every non-literal hostname, even an IPv4-only answer set | Native `:0`, no candidate discovery or added DNS lookup | Generic tcp/udp hostname resolution and address choice remain unchanged; all hostname exclusion susceptibility is a residual, not parity |

The prior “IPv4-only resolved hosts” adoption sentence is withdrawn. Wildcard sharing does not mean explicit IPv6 literals use randomized product selection or that IPv4 exclusion tables apply to IPv6.

Do not mutate Instance.port: it stays requested `:0` in Start error text/config state. Minimal private per-attempt port plumbing preserves fallback decisions based on the originally requested value. Both's complete TCP set must share a number before UDP acquisition; all partial sockets close on failure/reselection, and factory activation occurs only after a usable complete set. Up to 64 socket-set attempts; wildcard can bind twice per protocol, so report actual syscall tallies, not <=64 combined binds. Failed activation/Start/cancellation/Stop retain ownership/join behavior, UDP tuple eviction and accepted-TCP Stop semantics. UDP-only modes and packet.go remain unchanged, with their exclusion susceptibility an observation; no extra TCP bind/probe. Windows IPv6 product remains native while freeport ListenPair's separately certified Windows tcp6 randomized strategy stays unchanged.

**Acceptance:** deterministic shared-policy cases for TCP-only/UDP-only/overlapping exclusion sets, denied interval wider than 64 with usable complement, unavailable/empty/disjoint domain and sampling failure. UDP stale wrapped 10013/10048 retries within bounds and closes the complete TCP set; TCP wrapped 10013/10048 fails immediately with no UDP call; unrelated errors, wrapped invented Windows syscall.EADDRINUSE and Windows ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED fail fast with no UDP retry; fixed refusal/cancellation retain typed causes and requested diagnostics. Assert distinct bounded candidates, prefiltering, no more than 64 attempts, actual per-protocol syscall tallies, exactly-once full rollback and no failed-acquisition factory/state publication. Exercise explicit IPv4 adoption, explicit IPv6/zone native preservation, ALL non-literal hostname native preservation (IPv4-only and mixed answers without an added lookup), and wildcard matrix, full shared-port set, unavailable IPv6 and failed second-family/factory paths. Reusable Start after failure and cancellation/Stop closure are required. Run unchanged `TestBothListenersSharePort`, `TestBothListenersRetryEphemeralCollision`, `TestSharedUDPBindFaults`, both Windows TCP/UDP contracts, `TestBothProtocolStartStop`, `both_failure_test.go` factory/dual-stack rows, DNS ownership/seam/error rows and `packet_test.go` UDP lifecycle/error rows. Preserve DNS answer/TTL/TCP fallback/tuple isolation and reverse HTTPS. Map cases, t.Context, go-cmp and real peers; private selector/bind inputs may be injected, no mocked protocol services/new skips. The existing native manifest omits generic Both/Packet identities: replace the separate-invocation requirement with ONE additive modeserver row in the same unchanged native producer dispatch, selecting the complete names frozen below plus new ownership/rollback contracts. No second dispatch or new host/collector is authorized. New freeport top-level seam identities get ONE separate additive freeport row, preserving full-inventory/selected-union equality. Unit 1 owns row preparation/preflight; the credential-safe dispatcher owns dispatch; the independent verifier owns all raw identity/count audits. Native TestBothListenerDualStack must run without skip; an unavailable native IPv6 environment is incomplete acceptance, not permission to edit the existing test or count a skip as a pass.

Preserve immediate TCP error behavior in both adapters. Any helper API difference is explicit; parent production stays untouched. #### 2. Direct-netem terminal diagnosis only (NB-38)

**Retained trigger:** first attempts in runs `38147492398` and `38147524052` stop in the direct leg at 39059456 progress bytes with elapsed 30000857758 and 30000906002 ns; both retries passed (ratios 0.951532356926 and 0.948070054679). This is a baseline measured-transfer timeout, not evidence of a proxy throughput defect. The unit supplies missing causal/partial-byte diagnostics and a bounded source/evidence diagnosis report; it does not promise a root cause, remediation or closure of NB-16/NB-26.

**Receipt contract:** initialize bounded terminal state before listener/peer setup and register one owner-side sidecar publication before t.Cleanup or peer/socket closure. Write exactly one complete JSON SIDECAR per direct invocation under `NETWORK_GATE_OUTPUT`, on success and every setup/handshake/warmup/request/transfer/read/write/protocol/context/worker terminal path; never a new t.Log prefix, NETEM record or raw stdout. Use collision-free bounded per-invocation filenames inside the existing first/retry attempt output tree; no schema/body data or secret in the filename. Receipt collector/publisher is test-local and diagnostic-only; path is beneath the configured artifact root, one write per terminal outcome, with write failure explicit to the terminal certification/final receipt audit, not a silent missing-file pass or added NETEM diagnostic. Local non-hosted contracts set a test-owned output root. Include phase, stream (unavailable until known), expected bytes, actual received DATA-payload bytes, complete-frame validated payload bytes, last payload-progress offset, elapsed at the existing measurement boundary, outcome, original typed cause and race-safe snapshots of available client/origin failure/done/context/credit/progress state; mark uncreated/unmeasured fields unavailable. Actual counts partial DATA payload before error; validated counts complete frames accepted by the existing framer, not a new body validator. Raw wire bytes are separate, never mislabeled payload. A bounded diagnostic reader/accounting seam may supply partial-byte counts, with no response-body retention, per-chunk log, polling goroutine, blocking network operation or extra measured clock campaign. Capture n>0 before handling error and freeze elapsed before filesystem formatting/write. Preserve first cause over cleanup; snapshot uses consistent client→origin lock order and reads protected fields directly when locked, never calls bytes() or another locking helper under those mutexes. Retain protocol-over-timeout precedence and queued-at-deadline completion. Partial frame actual/validated values differ correctly; valid success has both equal expected. Successful peer parser/flow control/measured lifecycle and non-direct callers remain behavior-preserved; the following failed-construction fix is the only lifecycle exception. `newLoadHTTP2Peer` must distinguish reader-not-started from reader-owned done so cleanup never waits on p.done when no reader started; each acquired connection closes once. The direct origin goroutine hands accept/preface/settings/setup failure to the owner instead of FailNow before ready. The owner snapshots and publishes the original typed terminal cause before any teardown; it then cancels/joins only helpers actually started. Non-direct constructor callers retain their established success/error reporting contract without inheriting a wait on an unstarted reader.

**Classifier-untouched evidence transport (R50-2):** `hack/netem-gate.sh` is byte-identical, with no new prefix whitelist or extraction change. Existing `NETWORK_GATE_OUTPUT` uploads archive the sidecar before retry and after final attempt, via the current first/retry directories; scope the filename/path to match that retained tree. Sidecar content is audited independently and never an input to netem classification. Contract in `hack/network-gate_test.sh`: the same unchanged valid first-pass/direct-timeout/proxy-timeout/below-floor/protocol/unknown-diagnostic transcripts have identical classification/exit with sidecar absent or present, including a malformed sidecar; absence/malformed content does NOT become acceptance evidence merely because classifier is unchanged. Separate terminal certification rejects missing/duplicate/malformed/contradictory sidecars and proves pre-cleanup publication. Do not exploit ignored raw stdout, alter NETEM grammar, authorize a retry from a sidecar or suppress protocol/producer/tee/upload/restoration failures.

**Acceptance:** real-peer/subprocess certification covers pre-setup failure, preface-write failure, short/EOF/malformed-preface origin failure and handshake/warmup failure, stalled/no-progress and slow-progress timeout, partial DATA then typed error, RST_STREAM/GOAWAY/EOF, owner cancellation, concurrent first-cause publication, success and wrong stream/byte count. One complete sidecar precedes cleanup, original cause wins, elapsed is frozen and actual/validated accounting is correct. Use watchdog-backed subprocess failed-construction certification plus publication/snapshot race tests to detect unstarted-reader joins and recursive mutex deadlock; certify owner delivery of origin setup error before ready, original-cause pre-teardown sidecar, exactly-once acquired connection close, and cancellation/join of started helpers only. `TestLoadPeerLifecyclePreservation` certifies successful and non-direct callers. Padded-DATA and fragmented-header counter cases are optional certification, not round acceptance; they do not authorize parser/flow-control changes. All existing load assertions/FIN/intercept/peer behavior remain. Preserve netem shell contracts for first-pass, timeout→pass, below-floor→pass, second failure, protocol veto, malformed/unknown diagnostics and failed producer/tee/upload/restoration; add presence/absence invariance only, no classifier policy edit. Keep 256 MiB, 16 MiB windows, 0.8 floor, 30s measured wait, 5m peer context, 600s attempt and two attempts maximum. Existing NETEM timeout fields actual_bytes=unavailable remain unchanged; richer payload diagnosis is sidecar-only.

**Evidence:** retained originals supply the classified historical trigger, not a stochastic product-red requirement. Required parent-compatible subprocess assertions on bb31466 demonstrate missing sidecar diagnosis (test-hardening red, not throughput red); a missing helper compile error is not red. Candidate terminal and failed-construction contracts use the exact `h2load` selected matrix below, not the whole privileged tagged package; shared-peer and existing shell policy tests are preservation. New top-level identities are frozen as `TestLoadDirectTerminalSidecar`, `TestLoadDirectConstructionFailure`, `TestLoadDirectPayloadAccounting`, `TestLoadDirectSnapshotRace` and `TestLoadPeerLifecyclePreservation`, alongside existing `TestLoadNetemTimeoutDiagnostics` and `TestLoadNetemPeerDiagnostics`. None of these focused local contracts calls requireLoadNetem or requires tc/root; the real measured netem scenario remains in the unchanged hosted gate. Final frozen-head campaign uses 20 `network.yaml` dispatches on **20 distinct temporary refs at the same SHA**, separately archived; serial wait-for-terminal dispatch is the fallback if temporary refs cannot be used. No concurrent same-ref pending dispatches and no concurrency/job-name change. Audit ref→head→actual checkout mapping; retain first/eligible retry sidecar and ordinary NETEM receipt, producer/tee/restoration/upload statuses and per-invocation sidecar counts/digests. Each run retains seven core scenarios, complete Autobahn and two HTTP3 receipts; cancellations/missing artifacts or sidecars are incomplete, not pass. Report first-pass/retry-pass separately. Do not induce a hosted failure just to obtain a retry. Supply a bounded diagnosis report with race-safe peer-credit/progress/failure/context state and alternative hypotheses; a receipt-classified recurrence with a named mechanism opens a remediation unit next round. If no such recurrence/mechanism is established, NB-38 stays an owned observation after the instrumentation unit completes. Green jobs/retries never close it, and no proxy attribution, threshold change or invented root cause is allowed.

#### Executable focused command matrix

These are executor commands, not commands run during planning. Run from repository root with no cd. Shell setup: `R=/Users/zchee/go/src/github.com/zchee/mitmproxy-go; GATE="$R/.omc/artifacts/p49-session/with-gate-lock.sh"; OUT="$R/.omc/artifacts/p50-session/evidence/focused/$(git rev-parse HEAD)"; mkdir -p "$OUT"; export NETWORK_GATE_OUTPUT="$OUT"; export MITMPROXY_GO_HTTP2_LOAD=1`. Resolve GNU `timeout` (`/opt/homebrew/bin/timeout` on this host) before starting; it runs INSIDE the shared lock, so the 75m watchdog begins after acquisition. Each command writes complete raw stdout/stderr to its own `>|` log, records command-derived date start/end, SHA, host and exit, and rejects missing/empty inventory, selected skip, fail or truncated/unjoined terminal. Local subprocess certification uses a bounded inner watchdog; timed-out children are killed/joined and their logs retained, not counted as ordinary success. Existing elapsed/numeric load acceptance is unchanged. `Tags: none` means no -tags flag.

| Campaign / expected inventory | Exact command (all names complete) | Tags / count / cpu / test timeout / post-lock watchdog |
|---|---|---|
